Title: Travels through Syria and Egypt, in the years 1783, 1784, and 1785, containing the present natural and political state of those countries; translated from the French; Volume II; the second edition
Creator: C.-F. Volney (Constantin-Francois Chasseboeuf, comte de Volney, 1757-1820); translator not named on the title page
Publisher: London: printed for G. G. J. and J. Robinson, Pater-Noster-Row
Publication Date: 1788
Rights: Public Domain (published 1788, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Source: Internet Archive item travelsthroughsy02voln (https://archive.org/details/travelsthroughsy02voln), the item's own travelsthroughsy02voln_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: University of California Libraries; host metadata date: 1787. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: d7eb8fc42d1a2e41e332eff4ef545bb4cd692f1d95912325f1ed9fe6fcb618cd.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. University of California Libraries scan; clean OCR with the long s read as f. The title page reads 'The second edition' and MDCCLXXXVIII; the host dates the item 1787.
Content note: The second volume of Volney's account of Syria in the 1780s: the Druzes and Maronites of Lebanon, the Greek Catholic convents the Druzes allowed on their land, a description of the Melkite Basilian monastery of Mar Hanna at Shuwayr and its Arabic printing press with the monks' rule of life, then the pashaliks of Aleppo, Tripoli, Damascus and Acre and the state of the Christians in each.
